```
1	// 程序化世界生成管线：地形 → 洞穴 → 矿石 → 液体 → 表面 → 结构
2	// 分帧执行以显示进度。纯函数式 pass，每个 pass 直接写 TileStore（生成期用 silent 写入）。
3	import { createNoise2D } from 'simplex-noise';
4	import { RNG, hashString } from '../../core/rng';
5	import { T, W, TILE_DEFS, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../data/tiles';
6	import { World } from '../World';
7	
8	export interface GenConfig {
9	  width: number;
10	  height: number;
11	  seedText: string;
12	  name?: string;
13	  structures?: boolean;
14	  /** 邪恶群系：-1 随机（默认） 0 腐化 1 猩红（UIWorldCreation 对应原版 WorldGenParam_Evil） */
15	  evil?: -1 | 0 | 1;
16	  /** 每 pass 完成后回调部分世界（生成期实时地图预览用；无需等 finalize） */
17	  onWorldPartial?: (world: World, passIndex: number, passName: string) => void;
18	}
19	
20	export interface Pass {
21	  name: string;
22	  run: (ctx: GenCtx, report: (p: number) => void) => void;
23	}
24	
25	export interface GenCtx {
26	  world: World;
27	  store: World['store'];
28	  rng: RNG;
29	  noise2D: (x: number, y: number) => number;
30	  surface: Float32Array;   // 每列地表高度（tile y）
31	  cfg: GenConfig;
32	}
33	
34	/** 生成一个世界。passes 按序执行，每帧尽量做完一个 pass 后让出主线程。 */
35	export async function generateWorld(cfg: GenConfig, onProgress?: (label: string, p: number) => void): Promise<World> {
36	  const seed = hashString(cfg.seedText || String(Date.now()));
37	  const world = new World(cfg.width, cfg.height, seed, cfg.name ?? '新世界');
38	  const rng = new RNG(seed);
39	  const noise2D = createNoise2D(() => rng.next());
40	  const ctx: GenCtx = {
41	    world, store: world.store, rng, noise2D,
42	    surface: new Float32Array(cfg.width),
43	    cfg,
44	  };
45	
46	  // 原版管线:TerrainPass(五特征随机走) + TileRunner 泥石/洞穴;其余 pass 沿用
47	  // (lgcTerrain=false 走旧 fbm 地形作为回退开关)
48	  const useVanillaTerrain = (cfg as GenConfig & { lgcTerrain?: boolean }).lgcTerrain !== false;
49	  const passes: Pass[] = [
50	    ...(useVanillaTerrain ? [
51	      { name: '原版地形', run: vanillaTerrain },
52	      { name: '洞穴', run: vanillaCaves },
53	      { name: '海滩', run: vanillaBeaches },
54	      { name: '生物群系', run: vanillaBiomes },
55	      { name: '清理', run: vanillaCleanup },
56	      { name: '生命水晶', run: vanillaLifeCrystals },
57	    ] as Pass[] : [
58	      { name: '地形', run: terrainPass },
59	      { name: '洞穴', run: cavePass },
60	    ]),
61	    { name: '矿石', run: vanillaShinies },
62	    { name: '液体', run: vanillaLakes },
63	    ...(useVanillaTerrain ? [
64	      // 原版 "Smooth World"(L7568)+"Waterfalls"(L7697)：地表凸起与水边唇缘砸半砖
65	      // （半砖 = 原版水浸润/瀑布触发的核心，见 HalfBrickPass.ts）
66	      { name: '半砖平滑', run: halfBrickSmoothPass },
67	      { name: '瀑布唇缘', run: waterfallLipPass },
68	    ] as Pass[] : []),
69	    { name: '地狱', run: hellPass },
70	    ...((cfg as GenConfig & { lgcTerrain?: boolean }).lgcTerrain === false ? [{ name: '清浮空', run: floatCleanupPass }] : []),
71	    ...((cfg as GenConfig & { lgcTerrain?: boolean }).lgcTerrain === false ? [] : [{ name: '地狱屋', run: vanillaHellHouses }]),
72	    { name: '瓦罐', run: potPass },
73	    { name: '表面', run: surfacePass },
74	    ...((cfg as GenConfig & { lgcTerrain?: boolean }).lgcTerrain === false ? [] : [{ name: '地表装饰', run: vanillaSurfaceDecor }]),
75	    ...(cfg.structures === false ? [] : [{ name: '结构', run: structurePass }]),
76	  ];
77	
78	  for (let i = 0; i < passes.length; i++) {
79	    onProgress?.(passes[i].name, i / passes.length);
80	    await nextFrame();
81	    passes[i].run(ctx, () => {});
82	    // 部分世界回调（生成期实时预览）：pass 完成即暴露，不等 finalize
83	    if (cfg.onWorldPartial) cfg.onWorldPartial(world, i, passes[i].name);
84	  }
85	
86	  finalize(ctx);
87	  onProgress?.('完成', 1);
88	  return world;
89	}
90	
91	// ---------- 原版管线 pass(阶段 1-2 移植) ----------
92	import { newGenState, type GenState } from './vanilla/GenState';
93	import { runTerrainPass } from './vanilla/TerrainPass';
94	import { runRocksAndClayPass, runCavesPass } from './vanilla/CavesPass';
95	import { runOceanSandPass, runBeachesPass } from './vanilla/BeachPass';
96	import { runIceBiomePass } from './vanilla/IceBiomePass';
97	import { runGrassPass } from './vanilla/GrassPass';
98	import { runJunglePass } from './vanilla/JunglePass';
99	import { spreadGrassAll } from './vanilla/Spread';
100	import { runDesertPass } from './vanilla/DesertPass';
101	import { runMushroomPass } from './vanilla/MushroomPass';
102	import { runMarbleGranitePass } from './vanilla/MarbleGranitePass';
103	import { runDirtToMudAndSiltPass } from './vanilla/DirtToMudPass';
104	import { runCleanupPass } from './vanilla/CleanupPass';
105	import { runSmoothWorldPass, runWaterfallLipPass } from './vanilla/HalfBrickPass';
106	import { runShiniesPass, runGemsPass } from './vanilla/ShiniesPass';
107	import { runMountCavesPass, runMountainCavesPass } from './vanilla/MountCavesPass';
108	import { runTreePass, randomizeTreeStyle } from './vanilla/TreePass';
109	import { runLakesPass } from './vanilla/LakesPass';
110	import { runTrapsPass } from './vanilla/TrapsPass';
111	import { runFloatingIslandsPass, runLifeCrystalsPass, runSurfaceDecorPass, runPyramidPass, runWetJunglePass } from './vanilla/StructuresPass';
112	import { runBeehivePass, runSpiderNestPass } from './vanilla/HiveSpiderPass';
113	import { tileRunner } from './vanilla/TileRunner';
114	import { runDungeonPass } from './vanilla/DungeonPass';
115	import { runTemplePass } from './vanilla/TemplePass';
116	import { runIslandHousePass } from './vanilla/IslandHousePass';
117	import { runHellFortPass } from './vanilla/HellFortPass';
118	import { runSwordShrinePass } from './vanilla/SwordShrinePass';
119	import { runBuriedChestsPass, runSurfaceChestsPass, runWaterChestsPass } from './vanilla/BuriedChestsPass';
120	import { runCorruptionPass } from './vanilla/CorruptionPass';
121	import { placeDoorClosed } from '../Door';
122	
123	function vanillaTerrain({ store, rng, world, surface, cfg }: GenCtx) {
124	  const gs = newGenState(store.w, store.h);
125	  // Reset pass 掷骰(WorldGen.cs L4780-4880,掷骰顺序 = RNG 契约不可调换)
126	  // 矿石替代对:Next(2)==0 → 替代矿(内部 id)
127	  gs.oreTiers = {
128	    copper: rng.next() < 0.5 ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_tin']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_copper']!,
129	    iron: rng.next() < 0.5 ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_lead']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_iron']!,
130	    silver: rng.next() < 0.5 ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_tungsten']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_silver']!,
131	    gold: rng.next() < 0.5 ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_platinum']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_gold']!,
132	  };
133	  gs.crimson = rng.next() < 0.5;
134	  // RandomizeTreeStyle（原版 Reset L4837：worldID 之后）——树冠样式与边界
135	  const ts = randomizeTreeStyle(rng, store.w);
136	  gs.treeX = ts.treeX;
137	  gs.treeStyle = ts.treeStyle;
138	  // 邪恶群系覆盖（RNG 契约：骰照掷，仅覆盖结果）
139	  if (cfg.evil === 0) gs.crimson = false;
140	  else if (cfg.evil === 1) gs.crimson = true;
141	  world.crimson = gs.crimson;
142	  gs.dungeonSide = rng.next() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
143	  // 丛林:与地牢异侧(Next(15,30) 即 15-29)
144	  const jf = rng.int(15, 29) * 0.01;
145	  gs.jungleX = Math.floor(store.w * (gs.dungeonSide === -1 ? 1 - jf : jf));
146	  world.jungleX = gs.jungleX;
147	  // 地牢 X:拒绝采样直到落在地牢侧 15% 区间
148	  let dX = rng.int(0, store.w - 1);
149	  const dLo = store.w * (gs.dungeonSide === 1 ? 0.60 : 0.25);
150	  const dHi = store.w * (gs.dungeonSide === 1 ? 0.75 : 0.40);
151	  while (dX < dLo || dX > dHi) dX = rng.int(0, store.w - 1);
152	  gs.dungeonX = dX;
153	  // 雪原:以地牢 X 为中心向两侧扩展(L4863-4879)——原版雪原与地牢同侧
154	  const snowScale = store.w / 4200;
155	  const snowExtend = () => rng.int(50, 89) + Math.floor(rng.int(20, 39) * snowScale) + Math.floor(rng.int(20, 39) * snowScale);
156	  gs.snowOriginLeft = Math.max(0, dX - snowExtend());
157	  gs.snowOriginRight = Math.min(store.w, dX + snowExtend());
158	  // 海滩宽度(原版固定 300-340+档位加成,按 4200 宽设计;小世界线性缩放保持比例)
159	  const beachRoll = () => Math.max(20, Math.floor(rng.int(300, 339) * snowScale));
160	  gs.beachLeftEnd = beachRoll() + (gs.dungeonSide === 1 ? 40 : 20) * (snowScale >= 1 ? 1 : Math.max(0.3, snowScale));
161	  gs.beachRightStart = store.w - beachRoll() - (gs.dungeonSide === -1 ? 40 : 20) * (snowScale >= 1 ? 1 : Math.max(0.3, snowScale));
162	  // 地牢入口(L4891-4894):地牢侧海滩内 15% 区间随机
163	  if (gs.dungeonSide === -1) {
164	    gs.dungeonLocation = rng.int(gs.beachLeftEnd + 50, Math.floor(store.w * 0.2));
165	  } else {
166	    gs.dungeonLocation = rng.int(Math.floor(store.w * 0.8), gs.beachRightStart - 50);
167	  }
168	  world.dungeonX = gs.dungeonLocation;
169	  // Terrain pass
170	  runTerrainPass(store, rng, gs);
171	  // surface 数组给后续旧 pass(surfacePass 树木等)用:每列首个实心行
172	  for (let x = 0; x < store.w; x++) {
173	    for (let y = 0; y < store.h; y++) {
174	      if (store.flags[store.idx(x, y)]) { surface[x] = y; break; }
175	    }
176	  }
177	  ctxGs[0] = gs;
178	  // 世界级常量同步
179	  world.groundLevel = gs.worldSurface;
180	  world.rockLevel = gs.rockLevel;
181	  world.lavaLine = gs.lavaLine;
182	}
183	
184	function vanillaLakes({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
185	  // 原版 pass 31 Lakes（替换自研 liquidPass 洼地灌水/地下水池）
186	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
187	  if (!gs) return;
188	  runLakesPass(store, rng, gs);
189	}
190	
191	function vanillaShinies({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
192	  // 原版 pass 27 Shinies（替换自研 orePass）+ pass 36 Gems
193	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
194	  if (!gs) return;
195	  runShiniesPass(store, rng, gs);
196	  runGemsPass(store, rng, gs);
197	}
198	
199	function vanillaCaves({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
200	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
201	  if (!gs) return;
202	  runOceanSandPass(store, rng, gs); // 原版 pass 3(顺序上先于泥石/洞穴)
203	  if (gs) runMountCavesPass(store, rng, gs); // 原版 pass 7 Mount Caves(山丘本体)
204	  runRocksAndClayPass(store, rng, gs);
205	  runCavesPass(store, rng, gs);
206	}
207	
208	function vanillaBeaches({ store, rng, surface }: GenCtx) {
209	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
210	  if (!gs) return;
211	  runMountainCavesPass(store, rng, gs); // 原版 pass 34 Mountain Caves(山侧开口+蜿蜒,先于海滩)
212	  runBeachesPass(store, rng, gs);
213	  // 海盆挖完后刷新 surface[](后续旧 pass 依赖)
214	  for (let x = 0; x < store.w; x++) {
215	    for (let y = 0; y < store.h; y++) {
216	      if (store.flags[store.idx(x, y)]) { surface[x] = y; break; }
217	    }
218	  }
219	}
220	
221	// 原版 pass 15-22:冰原→草种→丛林→泥转丛林草→沙漠→蘑菇→大理石/花岗岩
222	function vanillaBiomes({ store, rng, world }: GenCtx) {
223	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
224	  if (!gs) return;
225	  runIceBiomePass(store, rng, gs);       // 15 Generate Ice Biome
226	  runGrassPass(store, rng, gs);          // 16 Grass
227	  const jx = runJunglePass(store, rng, gs); // JunglePass
228	  world.jungleX = jx;
229	  // 丛林表面泥化:泥柱核心区 ±0.12w 内,表层 10 格 dirt→mud(原版巨型 runner
230	  // 覆盖表面所致;我们补这一步让丛林表面成草皮而非裸土)
231	  {
232	    const core = (gs as any).jungleCoreX ?? jx;
233	    const reach = Math.floor(store.w * 0.12);
234	    for (let x = Math.max(0, core - reach); x < Math.min(store.w, core + reach); x++) {
235	      let sy = 0;
236	      while (sy < store.h - 12 && !store.flags[store.idx(x, sy)]) sy++;
237	      for (let y = sy; y < sy + 10; y++) {
238	        if (store.get(x, y) === T.DIRT) store.setTileSilent(x, y, TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!);
239	      }
240	    }
241	  }
242	  spreadGrassAll(store, TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!, TILE_BY_KEY['v_60_jungle_grass_block']!, world.jungleX, Math.floor(store.w * 0.21)); // 17 Mud Caves To Grass(丛林核心区)
243	  runDesertPass(store, rng, gs);         // 18 Full Desert
244	  runFloatingIslandsPass(store, rng, gs); // 19 Floating Islands
245	  runMushroomPass(store, rng, gs);       // 20 Mushroom Patches
246	  runMarbleGranitePass(store, rng, gs);  // 21/22 Marble/Granite
247	  runDirtToMudAndSiltPass(store, rng, gs); // 23 Dirt To Mud / 24 Silt
248	  runCorruptionPass(store, rng, gs);     // 28 Corruption/Crimson
249	  runDungeonPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 30 Dungeon(蓝/粉砖主题+走廊房间+宝箱)
250	  // 地牢 pass 预计算会重掷 dungeonLocation(锚点 ±300 拒绝采样后迁移)——必须在此后
251	  // 回写最终位置,否则 world.dungeonX 停留在 Reset 期的旧值(与实际地牢偏差可达数百格)
252	  world.dungeonX = gs.dungeonLocation;
253	  world.dungeonY = gs.dungeonY || 0;            // 入口 Y（0=未知，使用处回退 groundLevel）
254	  runPyramidPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 38 Pyramids(沙岩砖金字塔+宝箱)
255	  runBuriedChestsPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 35 Buried Chests(洞穴金/冰箱/沙箱+地狱箱)
256	  runSurfaceChestsPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 36 Surface Chests(地表木箱)
257	  runWaterChestsPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 39 Water Chests(水中 style17)
258	  runWetJunglePass(store, rng, gs);             // 43 Wet Jungle(丛林湿隧道灌水)
259	  runBeehivePass(store, rng, gs);               // 45 Hives(丛林蜂巢:蜂巢壳+蜂蜜+腔)
260	  runSpiderNestPass(store, rng, gs);            // 蜘蛛巢(蛛网+蜘蛛墙腔)
261	  runTemplePass(store, rng, gs, world.chests);  // 44 Jungle Temple(之字形房间链+走廊+门+宝箱)
262	  runIslandHousePass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 浮空岛日光板房屋(Skyware 门+箱+桌椅)
263	  runSwordShrinePass(store, rng, gs);          // 附魔剑圣地(竖井+水潭洞+土丘剑)
264	}

266	// 原版 pass 37 Clean Up Dirt:表层墙清理 + 连通小块(<20)清除
267	function vanillaCleanup({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
268	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
269	  if (!gs) return;
270	  runCleanupPass(store, rng, gs);
271	}

273	// 生命水晶(pass 55)——清完散块后放

277	  runLifeCrystalsPass(store, rng, gs);

280	// 原版 "Smooth World"(L7568)：地表凸起 → 半砖/削除（HalfBrickPass）
281	function halfBrickSmoothPass({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {

283	  if (!gs) return;

287	// 原版 "Waterfalls"(L7697)：水边唇缘砸半砖（瀑布触发来源）
288	function waterfallLipPass({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
289	  const gs = ctxGs[0];

294	// 地狱要塞 HellFort(5列×10行网格塔)——地狱层就绪后
295	function vanillaHellHouses({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {

301	// 生物群系感知地表装饰(替代旧 surfacePass 的种树部分)
302	function vanillaSurfaceDecor({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {

304	  if (!gs) return;

306	  runTrapsPass(store, rng, gs); // 原版 pass 76 Traps（地表装饰后、Piles 前）

309	// 原版 pass 与旧 pass 的桥接存储(单次生成生命周期)
310	const ctxGs: [GenState | null] = [null];
311	/** 探针/测试用：最近一次生成的 GenState（山丘坐标等） */
312	export function lastGenState(): GenState | null { return ctxGs[0]; }
313	
314	function nextFrame(): Promise<void> {
315	  return new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
316	}
317	
318	// ---------- 地形 ----------
319	function terrainPass({ store, surface, rng, cfg, noise2D }: GenCtx) {
320	  const { w, h } = store;
321	  const base = Math.floor(h * 0.28);
322	  // 多 octave 地表高度。平坦为主、偶发高峰：
323	  // 大势噪声幂次塑形（|v|^1.8）——噪声近 0 的大片区域被压平成平原，
324	  // 只有噪声较强处才隆起成峰；小起伏幅度减半避免处处尖刺
325	  const o1 = createNoise1Dish(rng, 0.012);
326	  const o2 = createNoise1Dish(rng, 0.05);
327	  const o3 = createNoise1Dish(rng, 0.11);
328	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
329	    const hill = (x > w * 0.18 && x < w * 0.82) ? 1 : 1.6; // 边缘更陡（近似海崖）
330	    const shape = (v: number, amp: number) => Math.sign(v) * Math.pow(Math.abs(v), 1.8) * amp;
331	    surface[x] = base + (shape(o1(x), 30) + o2(x) * 5 + o3(x) * 2) * hill;
332	  }
333	  // 左右海洋：最外侧 6% 下挖成海盆（岸边浅、世界缘深），后续灌水铺沙
334	  const oceanW = Math.max(24, Math.floor(w * 0.06));
335	  const seaLevel = base + 4; // 海平面（水表高度）
336	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
337	    const edge = Math.min(x, w - 1 - x);
338	    if (edge >= oceanW) continue;
339	    const t = 1 - edge / oceanW; // 0 岸边 → 1 世界缘
340	    const floorY = base + 2 + t * 16 + Math.sin(x * 0.3) * 1.5;
341	    surface[x] = Math.max(surface[x], Math.max(seaLevel + 2, floorY));
342	  }
343	  const rockLevel = Math.floor(h * 0.33);
344	  const nBlend = createNoise1Dish(rng, 0.05);
345	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
346	    const sy = Math.floor(surface[x]);
347	    // 每列岩石线带噪声起伏（±8 格），形成犬牙交错的土石过渡带
348	    const rl = rockLevel + nBlend(x) * 8;
349	    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
350	      if (y < sy) continue;
351	      let type: number = T.DIRT;
352	      if (y >= rl) type = T.STONE;
353	      else if (y > rl - 14) {
354	        // 过渡带：噪声混入石块夹层
355	        if (fbm(noise2D, x * 0.09, y * 0.09, 2) > 0.12) type = T.STONE;
356	      }
357	      store.setTileSilent(x, y, type);
358	      // 自然墙：地下土/石区背景
359	      if (y > sy + 3) store.setWallSilent(x, y, type === T.STONE ? W.STONE : W.DIRT);
360	    }
361	  }
362	  // 海洋灌水 + 沙滩铺沙：海盆内从海平面灌满水；盆底与近岸滩涂表层铺沙
363	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
364	    const edge = Math.min(x, w - 1 - x);
365	    const sy = Math.floor(surface[x]);
366	    if (edge < oceanW) {
367	      for (let y = seaLevel; y < sy; y++) store.setLiquid(x, y, 255, 1);
368	      for (let y = sy; y < sy + 5 && y < store.h; y++) {
369	        const t = store.get(x, y);
370	        if (t === T.DIRT || t === T.GRASS) store.setTileSilent(x, y, T.SAND);
371	      }
372	    } else if (edge < oceanW + 8 && surface[x] >= seaLevel - 1) {
373	      // 近岸滩涂：海平面附近的陆地表面沙化过渡
374	      for (let y = sy; y < sy + 4 && y < store.h; y++) {
375	        const t = store.get(x, y);
376	        if (t === T.DIRT || t === T.GRASS) store.setTileSilent(x, y, T.SAND);
377	      }
378	    }
379	    // 沙漠地下层：海洋区沙层之下换沙岩/硬化沙（5-14 格深处随机夹层）
380	    if (edge < oceanW) {
381	      const SANDSTONE = TILE_BY_KEY['sandstone']!, HARDSAND = TILE_BY_KEY['hardened_sand']!;
382	      for (let y = sy + 5; y < sy + 14 && y < store.h; y++) {
383	        const t = store.get(x, y);
384	        if ((t === T.DIRT || t === T.STONE) && rng.next() < 0.45) {
385	          store.setTileSilent(x, y, y < sy + 9 ? SANDSTONE : HARDSAND);
386	        }
387	      }
388	    }
389	  }
390	  // 雪原带：随机一侧的 10%-24% 区间 —— 地表雪化 + 浅层冰夹层（无树：surfacePass 只在草上种树）
391	  {
392	    const ICE = TILE_BY_KEY['ice']!;
393	    const snowLeft = rng.next() < 0.5;
394	    const xs0 = snowLeft ? Math.floor(w * 0.10) : Math.floor(w * 0.76);
395	    const xs1 = snowLeft ? Math.floor(w * 0.24) : Math.floor(w * 0.90);
396	    for (let x = xs0; x <= xs1; x++) {
397	      const sy = Math.floor(surface[x]);
398	      for (let y = sy; y < sy + 4 && y < store.h; y++) {
399	        const t = store.get(x, y);
400	        if (t === T.GRASS || t === T.DIRT) store.setTileSilent(x, y, T.SNOW);
401	      }
402	      for (let y = sy + 4; y < sy + 12 && y < store.h; y++) {
403	        if (store.get(x, y) === T.DIRT && rng.next() < 0.45) store.setTileSilent(x, y, ICE);
404	      }
405	    }
406	  }
407	  // 世界最底部 3 行基岩（不可破坏 → 用 STONE 代替，破坏规则由硬度统一处理，简化）
408	}
409	
410	// 伪 1D 噪声：借用 2D 噪声 y=0
411	function createNoise1Dish(rng: RNG, freq: number) {
412	  const n = createNoise2D(() => rng.next());
413	  return (x: number) => n(x * freq, 0);
414	}
415	
416	// ---------- 洞穴 ----------
417	function cavePass({ store, surface, noise2D, rng, cfg }: GenCtx) {
418	  const { w, h } = store;
419	  // 1) 噪声挖空（海洋列跳过：保护海床，防止海水灌进洞穴系统）
420	  const caveOceanW = Math.max(24, Math.floor(w * 0.06)) + 2;
421	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
422	    if (Math.min(x, w - 1 - x) < caveOceanW) continue;
423	    const sy = surface[x];
424	    for (let y = Math.floor(sy) + 6; y < h - 3; y++) {
425	      const depth = (y - sy) / (h - sy);
426	      const threshold = 0.30 - depth * 0.06; // 越深洞越多
427	      const v = fbm(noise2D, x * 0.028, y * 0.028, 3);
428	      if (v > threshold) store.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
429	    }
430	  }
431	  // 2) 蠕虫隧道（连通大洞穴）
432	  const worms = 16 + Math.floor(rng.range(0, 8));
433	  for (let i = 0; i < worms; i++) {
434	    let wx = rng.range(w * 0.08, w * 0.92);
435	    let wy = rng.range(h * 0.35, h * 0.92);
436	    let angle = rng.range(0, Math.PI * 2);
437	    const len = rng.int(60, 200);
438	    for (let s = 0; s < len; s++) {
439	      angle += (noise2D(s * 0.05, i * 10) * 0.6 + rng.range(-0.3, 0.3));
440	      wx += Math.cos(angle) * 1.2;
441	      wy += Math.sin(angle) * 0.8;
442	      if (wx < 4 || wx > w - 5 || wy < h * 0.25 || wy > h - 6) break;
443	      const r = 2.2 + noise2D(s * 0.03, i * 5) * 1.8;
444	      carveCircle(store, wx, wy, Math.max(1.2, r));
445	    }
446	  }
447	}
448	
449	function carveCircle(store: GenCtx['store'], cx: number, cy: number, r: number) {
450	  const x0 = Math.floor(cx - r), x1 = Math.ceil(cx + r);
451	  const y0 = Math.floor(cy - r), y1 = Math.ceil(cy + r);
452	  for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
453	    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
454	      if (!store.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
455	      const dx = x - cx, dy = y - cy;
456	      if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= r * r) store.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
457	    }
458	  }
459	}
460	
461	function fbm(n2: (x: number, y: number) => number, x: number, y: number, oct: number): number {
462	  let v = 0, amp = 1, freq = 1, norm = 0;
463	  for (let i = 0; i < oct; i++) {
464	    v += n2(x * freq, y * freq) * amp;
465	    norm += amp;
466	    amp *= 0.5; freq *= 2;
467	  }
468	  return v / norm; // -1..1
469	}
470	
471	// ---------- 地狱层(原版 Underworld pass,L5952,完整) ----------
472	// 1) 边界随机走(h-190..h-160),界上~20格灰烬,界下清空;
473	// 2) 岩浆线随机走(h-120..h-60),线以下空气灌岩浆;
474	// 3) 大灰烬丘陵(addTile 填充虚空)+ 横向灰烬团 + 挖掘隧道(填岩浆);
475	// 4) 底部 h-145/144 岩浆层 + 地狱石 58 矿脉
476	function hellPass({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
477	  const st = store;
478	  const ASH = T.ASH;
479	  const HELLSTONE = TILE_BY_KEY['ore_hellstone']!;
480	  // 1) 边界 + 灰烬带 + 界下清空
481	  let boundary = st.h - rng.int(150, 189);
482	  for (let x = 0; x < st.w; x++) {
483	    boundary += rng.int(-3, 3);
484	    if (boundary < st.h - 190) boundary = st.h - 190;
485	    if (boundary > st.h - 160) boundary = st.h - 160;
486	    const ashTop = boundary - 20 - rng.int(0, 2);
487	    for (let y = ashTop; y < st.h; y++) {
488	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
489	      if (y >= boundary) {
490	        st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
491	        st.liquid[i] = 0; st.liquidType[i] = 0;
492	      } else {
493	        st.type[i] = ASH; st.flags[i] = 1;
494	        st.wall[i] = 1;
495	      }
496	    }
497	  }
498	  // 2) 岩浆线
499	  let lavaY = st.h - rng.int(40, 69);
500	  for (let x = 10; x < st.w - 10; x++) {
501	    lavaY += rng.int(-10, 10);
502	    if (lavaY > st.h - 60) lavaY = st.h - 60;
503	    if (lavaY < st.h - 100) lavaY = st.h - 120;
504	    for (let y = lavaY; y < st.h - 10; y++) {
505	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
506	      if (!st.flags[i]) { st.liquid[i] = 255; st.liquidType[i] = 2; }
507	    }
508	  }
509	  const gs = ctxGs[0]!;
510	  // 3a) 大灰烬丘陵(1/50 列):巨型竖直灰烬 runner 填充虚空
511	  for (let x = 0; x < st.w; x++) {
512	    if (rng.next() * 50 >= 1) continue;
513	    let y = st.h - 65;
514	    while (!st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] && y > st.h - 135) y--;
515	    tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, rng.int(0, st.w - 1), y + rng.int(20, 49),
516	      rng.int(15, 19), 1000, ASH, true, 0, rng.int(1, 2), true);
517	  }
518	  // 3b) 每列 1/13:竖直灰烬柱 + 横向灰烬团 + 挖掘隧道(填岩浆)
519	  for (let x = 0; x < st.w; x++) {
520	    if (rng.next() * 13 >= 1) continue;
521	    let y = st.h - 65;
522	    while ((st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] > 0 || st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) && y > st.h - 140) y--;
523	    let scaleF = rng.int(1, 2);
524	    if (rng.next() * 3 < 1) scaleF *= 0.5;
525	    tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, x, y - rng.int(2, 4), rng.int(5, 29), 1000, ASH, true, 0, rng.int(1, 2), true);
526	    if (rng.next() < 0.5) {
527	      tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, x, y - rng.int(2, 4), Math.floor(rng.int(5, 14) * scaleF), Math.floor(rng.int(10, 14) * scaleF), ASH, true, 1, 0.3, false);
528	    }
529	    if (rng.next() < 0.5) {
530	      const s2 = rng.int(1, 2);
531	      tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, x, y - rng.int(2, 4), Math.floor(rng.int(5, 14) * s2), Math.floor(rng.int(10, 14) * s2), ASH, true, -1, 0.3, false);
532	    }
533	    tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, x + rng.int(-10, 9), y + rng.int(-10, 10), rng.int(5, 14), rng.int(5, 9), -2, false, rng.int(-1, 2), rng.int(-1, 2), false);
534	    if (rng.next() * 3 < 1) {
535	      tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, x + rng.int(-10, 9), y + rng.int(-10, 10), rng.int(10, 29), rng.int(10, 19), -2, false, rng.int(-1, 2), rng.int(-1, 2), false);
536	    }
537	    if (rng.next() * 5 < 1) {
538	      tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, x + rng.int(-15, 14), y + rng.int(-15, 9), rng.int(15, 29), rng.int(5, 19), -2, false, rng.int(-1, 2), rng.int(-1, 2), false);
539	    }
540	  }
541	  // 3c) 随机小挖掘(w 个)
542	  for (let n = 0; n < st.w; n++) {
543	    tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, rng.int(20, st.w - 21), rng.int(st.h - 180, st.h - 11),
544	      rng.int(2, 6), rng.int(2, 6), -2, false, 0, 0, false);
545	  }
546	  // 4) 底部岩浆层(h-145/144)+ 地狱石脉
547	  for (let x = 0; x < st.w; x++) {
548	    for (const dy of [145, 144]) {
549	      const i = st.idx(x, st.h - dy);
550	      if (!st.flags[i]) { st.liquid[i] = 255; st.liquidType[i] = 2; }
551	    }
552	  }
553	  for (let n = 0; n < st.w * st.h * 0.0008; n++) {
554	    tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, rng.int(0, st.w - 1), rng.int(st.h - 140, st.h - 1),
555	      rng.int(2, 6), rng.int(3, 6), HELLSTONE, false, 0, 0, false);
556	  }
557	}
558	
559	/** 地狱专用 runner:超界安全(地狱区常越界) */
560	function tileRunnerHell(st: World['store'], rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number,
561	  strength: number, steps: number, type: number, addTile: boolean,
562	  speedX: number, speedY: number, noYChange: boolean): void {
563	  tileRunner(st, rng, gs, i, Math.max(2, Math.min(st.h - 3, j)), {
564	    strength, steps, type, addTile, speedX, speedY, noYChange,
565	  });
566	}
567	
568	// ---------- 浮空方块清理 ----------
569	// 迭代剥离无支撑的孤立土/石块（下方三格全空且左右也空 → 移除），重复数次处理小簇
570	function floatCleanupPass({ store, surface }: GenCtx) {
571	  const st = store;
572	  for (let iter = 0; iter < 4; iter++) {
573	    let removed = 0;
574	    for (let x = 2; x < st.w - 2; x++) {
575	      const surfY = Math.floor(surface[x]);
576	      for (let y = surfY + 2; y < st.h - 2; y++) {
577	        const t = st.get(x, y);
578	        if (t !== T.DIRT && t !== T.STONE && t !== T.GRASS) continue;
579	        const below = st.isSolid(x, y + 1) || st.isSolid(x - 1, y + 1) || st.isSolid(x + 1, y + 1);
580	        const beside = st.isSolid(x - 1, y) || st.isSolid(x + 1, y);
581	        const above = st.isSolid(x, y - 1);
582	        if (!below && !beside && !above) {
583	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
584	          removed++;
585	        }
586	      }
587	    }
588	    if (removed === 0) break;
589	  }
590	}
591	
592	// ---------- 瓦罐散布 ----------
593	function potPass({ store, surface, rng }: GenCtx) {
594	  const { w, h } = store;
595	  const POT = TILE_BY_KEY['pot']!;
596	  // 尝试次数随世界宽度；洞穴/浅层洞的地面（下方实心、上方 2x2 净空）放 1-3 个一组
597	  const tries = Math.floor(w / 6);
598	  for (let i = 0; i < tries; i++) {
599	    const x = rng.int(4, w - 5);
600	    const yMin = Math.floor(surface[x]) + 8;
601	    const yMax = Math.min(h - 6, Math.floor(surface[x]) + Math.floor(h * 0.45));
602	    if (yMax <= yMin) continue;
603	    const y = rng.int(yMin, yMax);
604	    // 2x2 净空 + 底部实心 = 洞内地面
605	    let ok = true;
606	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 2 && ok; dx++) {
607	      for (let dy = 1; dy <= 2; dy++) {
608	        if (store.isActive(x + dx, y - dy)) { ok = false; break; }
609	      }
610	    }
611	    if (!ok || !store.isSolid(x, y) || !store.isSolid(x + 1, y)) continue;
612	    // 该点 12 格内有其他瓦罐则跳过（避免扎堆）
613	    let nearby = false;
614	    for (let dx = -6; dx <= 6 && !nearby; dx++) {
615	      for (let dy = -4; dy <= 2; dy++) {
616	        if (store.get(x + dx, y + dy) === POT) { nearby = true; break; }
617	      }
618	    }
619	    if (nearby) continue;
620	    // 放 1-3 个（间距 2-3 格，各自需独立净空）
621	    const n = rng.int(1, 3);
622	    let px = x;
623	    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
624	      if (store.get(px, y - 1) === 0 && store.get(px + 1, y - 1) === 0 &&
625	          store.get(px, y - 2) === 0 && store.get(px + 1, y - 2) === 0 && store.isSolid(px, y)) {
626	        store.setTileSilent(px, y - 1, POT, 0, 0);
627	        store.setTileSilent(px + 1, y - 1, POT, 18, 0);
628	        store.setTileSilent(px, y - 2, POT, 0, 18);
629	        store.setTileSilent(px + 1, y - 2, POT, 18, 18);
630	      }
631	      px += rng.int(2, 4);
632	      if (px > w - 4) break;
633	    }
634	  }
635	}
636	
637	// ---------- 矿石 ----------
638	// 已由 vanilla/ShiniesPass.ts（原版 pass 27/36）整体替换，旧 orePass/growOreBlob 删除
639	
640	// ---------- 液体 ----------
641	// 已由 vanilla/LakesPass.ts（原版 pass 31）替换，旧 liquidPass 删除
642	
643	// ---------- 表面 ----------
644	function surfacePass({ store, surface, rng, world }: GenCtx) {
645	  const { w } = store;
646	  // 铺草（原版 pass 73 Spreading Grass 尾段 L8731-8755：每列首个实心格为泥土 → SpreadGrass）
647	  // 旧实现用生成早期 surface[] 数组定位列顶——山坡/洞穴口露石露土全漏，树密度被卡死
648	  for (let x = 10; x < w - 10; x++) {
649	    for (let y = 0; y < world.groundLevel - 1; y++) {
650	      const i = store.idx(x, y);
651	      if (!store.flags[i]) continue;
652	      if (store.type[i] === T.DIRT) store.setTileSilent(x, y, T.GRASS);
653	      break; // 只处理首个实心格
654	    }
655	  }
656	  // 原版 pass 82 Planting Trees（铺草之后、杂草之前——原版 73<82<86 顺序）
657	  {
658	    const gs = ctxGs[0];
659	    if (gs) runTreePass(store, rng, gs);
660	  }
661	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
662	    const sy = Math.floor(surface[x]);
663	    // 装饰：草丛/花/蘑菇——放在草块上方空气格（sy 是首个实心行，装饰在 sy-1）
664	    // 帧为 Tiles_3 表的像素偏移（TEdit frames 数据）：草丛 6 变体 / 花 9 色 / 蘑菇 (144,0)
665	    if (sy - 1 >= 0 && !store.isActive(x, sy - 1) && store.isSolid(x, sy)) {
666	      const GRASS_FX = [0, 18, 36, 54, 72, 90];
667	      const FLOWER_FX = [108, 126, 162, 180, 216, 252, 342, 540, 594];
668	      const r = rng.next();
669	      if (r < 0.34) store.setTileSilent(x, sy - 1, T.TALLGRASS, GRASS_FX[Math.floor(rng.next() * GRASS_FX.length) % 6], 0);
670	      else if (r < 0.48) {
671	        store.setTileSilent(x, sy - 1, T.FLOWER, FLOWER_FX[Math.floor(rng.next() * FLOWER_FX.length) % 9], 0);
672	        // 花簇：50% 概率向两侧蔓延 1 格（同簇同帧）
673	        const fx = store.frameX[store.idx(x, sy - 1)];
674	        for (const dx of [-1, 1]) {
675	          if (rng.next() < 0.5 && store.get(x + dx, sy - 1) === 0 && store.isSolid(x + dx, sy)) {
676	            store.setTileSilent(x + dx, sy - 1, T.FLOWER, fx, 0);
677	          }
678	        }
679	      }
680	      else if (r < 0.51) store.setTileSilent(x, sy - 1, T.MUSHROOM, 144, 0);
681	    }
682	  }
683	  // 树已由 vanilla/TreePass.ts（原版 pass 82 AddTrees/GrowTree/GrowPalmTree/GrowEpicTree）替换
684	}
685	
686	// ---------- 结构（地下小屋 + 宝箱） ----------
687	function placeChest(store: GenCtx['store'], x: number, y: number) {
688	  // 2×2 宝箱 object（锚点在左上）
689	  store.setTileSilent(x, y, T.CHEST, 0, 0);
690	  store.setTileSilent(x + 1, y, T.CHEST, 1, 0);
691	  store.setTileSilent(x, y + 1, T.CHEST, 0, 1);
692	  store.setTileSilent(x + 1, y + 1, T.CHEST, 1, 1);
693	}
694	
695	function structurePass({ store, surface, rng, world }: GenCtx) {
696	  const { w, h } = store;
697	  const hutCount = 4;
698	  for (let i = 0; i < hutCount; i++) {
699	    const hx = rng.int(20, w - 30);
700	    const hy = rng.int(Math.floor(h * 0.45), h - 40);
701	    buildHut(store, hx, hy, rng);
702	    // 小屋中心宝箱（坐在地板上：地板在 hy+6）
703	    placeChest(store, hx + 4, hy + 4);
704	    world.chests.push({ x: hx + 4, y: hy + 4, items: Array(10).fill(null) });
705	  }
706	  // 地表/浅层散落宝箱
707	  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
708	    const cx = rng.int(10, w - 12);
709	    // 放在洞穴空地的地上（需要 2×2 空间）
710	    for (let y = Math.floor(surface[cx]) + 8; y < h - 10; y++) {
711	      const clear =
712	        !store.isActive(cx, y) && !store.isActive(cx + 1, y) &&
713	        !store.isActive(cx, y - 1) && !store.isActive(cx + 1, y - 1) &&
714	        !store.isActive(cx, y + 1) && !store.isActive(cx + 1, y + 1);
715	      if (clear && store.isSolid(cx, y + 2) && store.isSolid(cx + 1, y + 2)) {
716	        placeChest(store, cx, y);
717	        world.chests.push({ x: cx, y, items: Array(10).fill(null) });
718	        break;
719	      }
720	    }
721	  }
722	}
723	
724	function buildHut(store: GenCtx['store'], hx: number, hy: number, rng: RNG) {
725	  const rw = 9, rh = 6;
726	  // 清空内部
727	  for (let x = hx; x < hx + rw; x++) {
728	    for (let y = hy; y < hy + rh; y++) {
729	      store.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
730	      store.setWallSilent(x, y, W.WOOD);
731	    }
732	  }
733	  // 地板与墙
734	  for (let x = hx - 1; x <= hx + rw; x++) {
735	    store.setTileSilent(x, hy + rh, T.WOOD);
736	    store.setTileSilent(x, hy - 1, T.WOOD);
737	  }
738	  for (let y = hy; y <= hy + rh; y++) {
739	    store.setTileSilent(hx - 1, y, T.WOOD);
740	    store.setTileSilent(hx + rw, y, T.WOOD);
741	  }
742	  // 门洞（左侧地板上方 3 格）
743	  for (let y = hy + rh - 3; y < hy + rh; y++) store.setTileSilent(hx - 1, y, 0);
744	  placeDoorClosed(store, hx - 1, hy + rh - 3, 0);
745	  // 内部陈设
746	  store.setTileSilent(hx + 1, hy + rh - 1, T.WORKBENCH, 0, 0);
747	  store.setTileSilent(hx + 3, hy + rh - 1, T.TORCH, 0, 0);
748	  if (rng.chance(0.5)) store.setTileSilent(hx + 5, hy + rh - 1, T.FURNACE, 0, 0);
749	}
750	
751	// ---------- 收尾：出生点（选最平坦区域并整平） ----------
752	function finalize({ store, surface, world, cfg, rng }: GenCtx) {
753	  // 幽灵净化:生成期 TileRunner 会在空气格写残留 type(原版语义,渲染不可见),
754	  // 但我们的存档格式无 active 位——进入存档前归零(防重读激活成浮空块)
755	  for (let i = 0; i < store.type.length; i++) {
756	    if (!store.flags[i] && store.type[i] !== 0) store.type[i] = 0; // 幽灵 type
757	    if (store.type[i] === 0) store.flags[i] = 0; // 幻影活性(type 0 不可有 flags)
758	  }
759	  // ---- 原版 pass 78 Spawn Point（cs:9471-9508）：中心随机扩张采样，自然地表，不改地形 ----
760	  // 旧实现为"找最平坦窗口+人工整平平台"——出生点是一块突兀平台，与原版自然地表不符
761	  let range = 5;
762	  let sx = Math.floor(store.w / 2), sy = 0;
763	  for (;;) {
764	    const x = Math.floor(store.w / 2) + rng.int(-range, range); // Next(-num2, num2+1)
765	    for (let y = 0; y < store.h; y++) {
766	      if (store.flags[store.idx(x, y)]) { sx = x; sy = y; break; }
767	    }
768	    let bad = false;
769	    range++;
770	    const gs2 = ctxGs[0];
771	    if (gs2 && sy > gs2.worldSurface) bad = true;
772	    if (store.liquid[store.idx(sx, Math.max(0, sy - 1))] > 0) bad = true;
773	    if (!bad) break;
774	  }
775	  let widen = 10;
776	  const gs0 = ctxGs[0];
777	  while (gs0 && sy > gs0.worldSurface) {
778	    const x = rng.int(Math.floor(store.w / 2) - widen, Math.floor(store.w / 2) + widen);
779	    for (let y = 0; y < store.h; y++) {
780	      if (store.flags[store.idx(x, y)]) { sx = x; sy = y; break; }
781	    }
782	    widen++;
783	  }
784	  world.spawnX = sx;
785	  world.spawnY = Math.max(4, sy - 3);
786	  // 出生点上空净空保留（防卡块；不整平地形）
787	  for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
788	    for (let dy = 1; dy <= 3; dy++) {
789	      const i = store.idx(sx + dx, sy - dy);
790	      if (store.flags[i]) store.setTileSilent(sx + dx, sy - dy, 0);
791	    }
792	  }
793	
794	  // 原版管线的地表/岩石线来自 TerrainPass 输出,旧 fbm 常数仅作回退
795	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
796	  if (gs) {
797	    world.groundLevel = gs.worldSurface;
798	    world.rockLevel = gs.rockLevel;
799	  } else {
800	    world.groundLevel = Math.floor(store.h * 0.28);
801	    world.rockLevel = Math.floor(store.h * 0.33);
802	  }
803	  // 原版树样式（RandomizeTreeStyle 掷出：边界 + 互异样式）
804	  if (gs) {
805	    world.treeX = gs.treeX;
806	    world.treeStyle = gs.treeStyle;
807	    world.trees = gs.genTrees; // 砍伐整棵判定（Player/Game 消费）
808	  }
809	  // 出生点上空净空
810	  for (let dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++) {
811	    for (let dy = 1; dy <= 5; dy++) {
812	      if (store.isActive(sx + dx, sy - dy)) {
813	        const t = store.get(sx + dx, sy - dy);
814	        if (t !== T.GRASS) store.setTileSilent(sx + dx, sy - dy, 0);
815	      }
816	    }
817	  }
818	  // 清除整平区内登记的树（防幽灵树）
819	  world.trees = world.trees.filter((t) => Math.abs(t.x - sx) > 5);
820	}
```

```
265	    }
266	  }
267	  return true;

275	  }
276	  return _oreSet.has(t);

280	export function runTrapsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
281	  const trapTries = Math.floor(st.w * 0.05);
282	  for (let n = 0; n < trapTries; n++) {
283	    for (let k = 0; k < 1150; k++) {
284	      let x = rng.int(200, st.w - 201);                       // Next(200, w-200)
285	      let y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface, st.h - 211);           // Next(surface, h-210)
286	      // oceanDepths 回避
287	      const oceanTop = (gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2 + 40;
288	      const beachL = gs.beachLeftEnd, beachR = st.w - gs.beachRightStart;
289	      while (y <= oceanTop && (x < beachL || x > beachR)) {
290	        x = rng.int(200, st.w - 201);
291	        y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface, st.h - 211);
292	      }
293	      if (st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] === 0 && placeTrap(st, rng, gs, x, y, -1)) break;
294	    }
295	  }
296	  const sandTries = Math.floor(st.w * 0.003);
297	  for (let n = 0; n < sandTries; n++) {
298	    for (let k = 0; k < 20000; k++) {
299	      const x = rng.int(Math.floor(st.w * 0.15), Math.floor(st.w * 0.85) - 1);
300	      const y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface + 20, st.h - 211);
301	      if (st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] === 187 && placeSandTrap(st, rng, x, y)) break;
302	    }
303	  }
304	}
```

```
116	  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i - 2, i + 2, index1 - clear, index1 - 1)) return false;

138	function growPalmTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, i: number, y: number): boolean {
139	  if (!st.inBounds(i, y)) return false;
140	  let index1 = y;

146	  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i, i, index1 - 2, index1 - 1)) return false;
147	  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i - 1, i + 1, index1 - 30, index1 - 3)) return false;
148	  const height = rng.int(10, 20);                 // Next(10,21)
149	  const target = rng.int(-8, 8) * 2;              // Next(-8,9)*2
150	  let tilt = 0;
151	  for (let k = 0; k < height; k++) {
152	    if (k === 0) {

154	      continue;
155	    }
156	    if (k === height - 1) {

158	      continue;
159	    }
160	    if (tilt !== target) {
161	      const prog = k / height;                    // 倾斜步进阈值（L11814-11820）
162	      if (prog >= 0.25 && ((prog < 0.5 && rng.int(0, 12) === 0)
163	        || (prog < 0.7 && rng.int(0, 8) === 0)
164	        || (prog < 0.95 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) || true)) {
165	        tilt += Math.sign(target) * 2;
166	      }
167	    }
168	    st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1 - k, PALM, 22 * rng.int(0, 2), tilt); // fx=22*Next(0,3)
169	  }
170	  return true;
171	}

173	/** AddTrees（L15642）：逐列尝试（~1.6 列间距）；沙滩棕榈按每侧固定概率；

175	export function runTreePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
176	  // EpicTree 林区（pass 82 头段，L9656-9666）：w*0.003 个 25-50 半宽林区逐格尝试
177	  for (let n = 0; n < st.w * 0.003; n++) {
178	    const cx = rng.int(50, st.w - 51);            // Next(50, maxTilesX-50)
179	    const hw = rng.int(25, 49);                  // Next(25, 50)
180	    for (let x = cx - hw; x < cx + hw; x++) {
181	      for (let y = 20; y < gs.worldSurface; y++) growEpicTree(st, rng, gs, x, y);
182	    }
183	  }
184	  const palmL = 0.1 + rng.next() * 0.35;          // 每侧棕榈概率（L15644-45）
185	  const palmR = 0.1 + rng.next() * 0.35;
186	  for (let x = 1; x < st.w - 1; x++) {
187	    for (let y = 20; y < gs.worldSurface; y++) {
188	      if (x < 380) {
189	        if (rng.next() < palmL && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] === 0) growPalmTree(st, rng, x, y);
190	      } else if (x > st.w - 380) {
191	        if (rng.next() < palmR && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] === 0) growPalmTree(st, rng, x, y);
192	      }
193	      growTree(st, rng, gs, x, y);
194	    }
195	    if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) x++;                 // Next(3)==0 跳列
196	    if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) x++;                 // Next(4)==0 跳列
197	  }
198	}
199	
200	/** RandomizeTreeStyle（L2817）：按世界宽掷 treeX 边界与 treeStyle（互异，style0→4 概率 2/3）。
201	 *  Reset pass 调用（L4837，worldID 之后 dungeonSide 之前） */
202	export function randomizeTreeStyle(rng: RNG, w: number): { treeX: number[]; treeStyle: number[] } {
203	  const treeX = [0, 0, 0], treeStyle = [0, 0, 0, 0];
204	  const roll6 = () => rng.int(0, 5);
205	  const fix0 = (arr: number[]) => {
206	    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
207	      if (arr[i] === 0 && rng.int(0, 2) !== 0) arr[i] = 4; // style0 → 4（2/3）
208	    }
209	  };
210	  if (w === 4200) {
211	    treeX[0] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.5 - w * 0.25), Math.floor(w * 0.5 + w * 0.25) - 1);
212	    treeStyle[0] = roll6(); treeStyle[1] = roll6();
213	    while (treeStyle[1] === treeStyle[0]) treeStyle[1] = roll6();
214	    treeX[1] = w; treeX[2] = w;
215	    fix0([treeStyle[0], treeStyle[1]]);
216	  } else if (w === 6400) {
217	    treeX[0] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.334 - w * 0.2), Math.floor(w * 0.334 + w * 0.2) - 1);
218	    treeX[1] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.667 - w * 0.2), Math.floor(w * 0.667 + w * 0.2) - 1);
219	    treeStyle[0] = roll6(); treeStyle[1] = roll6(); treeStyle[2] = roll6();
220	    while (treeStyle[1] === treeStyle[0]) treeStyle[1] = roll6();
221	    while (treeStyle[2] === treeStyle[0] || treeStyle[2] === treeStyle[1]) treeStyle[2] = roll6();
222	    treeX[2] = w;
223	    fix0([treeStyle[0], treeStyle[1], treeStyle[2]]);
224	  } else {
225	    treeX[0] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.25 - w * 0.15), Math.floor(w * 0.25 + w * 0.15) - 1);
226	    treeX[1] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.5 - w * 0.15), Math.floor(w * 0.5 + w * 0.15) - 1);
227	    treeX[2] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.75 - w * 0.15), Math.floor(w * 0.75 + w * 0.15) - 1);
228	    treeStyle[0] = roll6(); treeStyle[1] = roll6(); treeStyle[2] = roll6(); treeStyle[3] = roll6();
229	    while (treeStyle[1] === treeStyle[0]) treeStyle[1] = roll6();
230	    while (treeStyle[2] === treeStyle[0] || treeStyle[2] === treeStyle[1]) treeStyle[2] = roll6();
231	    while (treeStyle[3] === treeStyle[0] || treeStyle[3] === treeStyle[1] || treeStyle[3] === treeStyle[2]) treeStyle[3] = roll6();
232	    fix0(treeStyle);
233	  }
234	  return { treeX, treeStyle };
235	}
```

```
    public static bool placeTrap(int x2, int y2, int type = -1)
    {
      int index1 = x2;
      int j1 = y2;
      bool flag1 = false;
      bool flag2 = false;
      while (!WorldGen.SolidTile(index1, j1, false))
      {
        ++j1;
        if (j1 > Main.maxTilesY - 10)
          return false;
        if (j1 >= Main.maxTilesY - 300)
          flag2 = true;
      }
      int index2 = j1 - 1;
      if (WorldGen.IsTileNearby(index1, index2, 70, 20) || Main.tile[index1, index2].wall == (ushort) 87)
        return false;
      if (Main.tile[index1, index2].liquid > (byte) 0 && Main.tile[index1, index2].lava())
        flag1 = true;
      if (type == -1 && WorldGen.genRand.Next(20) == 0)
        type = 2;
      else if (type == -1 && index2 > WorldGen.lavaLine + 30 && WorldGen.genRand.Next(6) != 0)
        type = 3;
      else if (type == -1)
        type = WorldGen.genRand.Next(2);
      if (!WorldGen.InWorld(index1, index2, 3) || flag1 && type != 3 || (flag2 && type != 3 || (Main.tile[index1, index2].nactive() || Main.tile[index1 - 1, index2].nactive())) || (Main.tile[index1 + 1, index2].nactive() || Main.tile[index1, index2 - 1].nactive() || (Main.tile[index1 - 1, index2 - 1].nactive() || Main.tile[index1 + 1, index2 - 1].nactive()) || (Main.tile[index1, index2 - 2].nactive() || Main.tile[index1 - 1, index2 - 2].nactive() || (Main.tile[index1 + 1, index2 - 2].nactive() || Main.tile[index1, index2 + 1].type == (ushort) 48))) || Main.tile[index1, index2 + 1].type == (ushort) 232)
        return false;
      if (type == 1)
      {
        for (int index3 = index1 - 3; index3 <= index1 + 3; ++index3)
        {
```

```
24	export function runFloatingIslandsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
25	  const { w } = st;
26	  // 原版 pass 21（L5552-5637）：岛数 = w*0.0008，另加 skyLakes（1 + w>8000 + w>6000）。
27	  // 前 num2 个 = CloudIsland，其余 = CloudLake；间距/中心避让为固定值（不缩放）
28	  const islands = Math.floor(w * 0.0008);
29	  let skyLakes = 1;
30	  if (w > 8000) skyLakes++;
31	  if (w > 6000) skyLakes++;
32	  const total = islands + skyLakes;
33	  const placed: number[] = [];
34	  let lakeIdx = 0;
35	  for (let n = 0; n < total; n++) {
36	    let x = 0, ok = false;
37	    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 200 && !ok; attempt++) {
38	      x = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.1), Math.floor(w * 0.9) - 1);
39	      while (x > w / 2 - 150 && x < w / 2 + 150) {
40	        x = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.1), Math.floor(w * 0.9) - 1);
41	      }
42	      ok = placed.every((px) => Math.abs(px - x) >= 180);
43	    }
44	    // 该列地表（原版从 y=200 起扫）
45	    let surf = 0;
46	    let found = false;
47	    for (let y = 200; y < gs.worldSurface; y++) {
48	      if (st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) { surf = y; found = true; break; }
49	    }
50	    if (!found) continue;
51	    const y = Math.min(rng.int(90, Math.max(91, surf - 100)), gs.worldSurfaceLow - 50);
52	    const isLake = lakeIdx >= islands;
53	    if (isLake) cloudLake(st, rng, x, y);
54	    else cloudIsland(st, rng, x, y);

56	    gs.floatingIslands.push({ x, y, style: 0, isLake });

199	          if (!st.inBounds(x + ax, y + ay) || !st.flags[st.idx(x + ax, y + ay)]) { all = false; break; }
200	        }
201	      }
202	      if (all) st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] = 73;
203	    }
204	  }
205	  // ---- 阶段6:水池(每列 10% 在云顶凹处放水)----
206	  for (let x = bboxX0 + 2; x < bboxX1 - 2; x++) {
207	    if (rng.next() >= 0.1) continue;
208	    let y = Math.max(1, bboxY0 - 10);
209	    while (y < st.h - 1 && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) y++;
210	    const below = st.idx(x, y + 1);
211	    if (y < bboxY1 && st.type[below] === CLOUD) {
212	      const ti = st.idx(x, y);
213	      st.type[ti] = 0; st.flags[ti] = 0;
214	      st.liquid[ti] = 255; st.liquidType[ti] = 1;
215	    }
216	  }

219	/** 生命水晶(pass 55):area×2e-5,岩层空腔地面 */
220	export function runLifeCrystalsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
221	  const { w, h } = st;
222	  const yMin = Math.floor((gs.worldSurface * 2 + gs.rockLevel) / 3);
223	  const count = Math.floor(w * h * 0.00002);
224	  let placed = 0;
225	  for (let n = 0; n < count * 30 && placed < count; n++) {
226	    const x = rng.int(40, w - 41);
227	    const y = rng.int(yMin, Math.max(yMin + 1, h - 300));
228	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y + 1) || !st.inBounds(x + 1, y + 1)) continue;
229	    // 2×2 净空 + 下方实心
230	    let clear = true;
231	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
232	      for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
233	        if (st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, y - dy)]) { clear = false; break; }
234	      }
235	    }
236	    if (!clear || !st.isSolid(x, y + 1) || !st.isSolid(x + 1, y + 1)) continue;
237	    // 原版 AddLifeCrystal(cs:16023-16038):顶行 frameY=0、底行 18(此前写反致贴图上下颠倒)
238	    st.setTileSilent(x, y - 1, HEART, 0, 0);
239	    st.setTileSilent(x + 1, y - 1, HEART, 18, 0);
240	    st.setTileSilent(x, y, HEART, 0, 18);
241	    st.setTileSilent(x + 1, y, HEART, 18, 18);
242	    placed++;
243	  }
244	}

246	/** 地狱屋(L16085 简化):地狱中段黑曜石砖小屋(空心 + 砖墙背景) */
247	export function runHellHousesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
248	  const { w, h } = st;
249	  const hellTop = h - 200; // 地狱层顶部(Underworld 边界 h-190..h-160)
250	  if (hellTop >= h - 60) return;
251	  const count = Math.max(2, Math.floor(w / 4200 * 6)); // C# (int)
252	  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
253	    const hx = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.15), Math.floor(w * 0.85) - 14);
254	    const hy = rng.int(hellTop + 20, h - 90);
255	    const rw = rng.int(10, 16), rh = rng.int(5, 7);
256	    // 空心 + 墙
257	    for (let x = hx; x < hx + rw; x++) {
258	      for (let y = hy; y < hy + rh; y++) {
259	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
260	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
261	        st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
262	        st.wall[i] = 14; // HellstoneBrickUnsafe
263	      }
264	    }
265	    // 砖壳
266	    for (let x = hx - 1; x <= hx + rw; x++) {
267	      for (let y = hy - 1; y <= hy + rh; y++) {
268	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
269	        const border = x === hx - 1 || x === hx + rw || y === hy - 1 || y === hy + rh;
270	        if (border) {
271	          const i = st.idx(x, y);
272	          st.type[i] = rng.next() < 0.8 ? OBSIDIAN_BRICK : HELL_BRICK;
273	          st.flags[i] = 1;
274	          st.liquid[i] = 0;
275	        }
276	      }
277	    }
278	    // 开口(左右各去 2 砖)
279	    for (let y = hy + rh - 3; y <= hy + rh; y++) {
280	      for (const dx of [hx - 1, hx + rw]) {
281	        if (st.inBounds(dx, y)) {
282	          const i = st.idx(dx, y);
283	          st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
284	        }
285	      }
286	    }
287	    // 灰烬基座(原版房屋坐落在地狱灰烬地表上,我们补一座墩)
288	    const ash = T.ASH;
289	    for (let x = hx - 1; x <= hx + rw; x++) {
290	      for (let y = hy + rh + 1; y < Math.min(st.h, hy + rh + 30); y++) {
291	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
292	        if (st.flags[i]) break;
293	        st.type[i] = ash; st.flags[i] = 1;
294	      }
295	    }
296	  }
297	}

299	/** 地表装饰:按生物群系种树/仙人掌/藤蔓/蘑菇(原版 pass 60+ 系列简化) */
300	export function runSurfaceDecorPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
301	  const { w, h } = st;
302	  const isSoft = (t: number) => t === T.TALLGRASS || t === T.FLOWER || t === T.MUSHROOM || t === VINE;
303	  const ud = gs.undergroundDesert;
304	  const inDesert = (x: number) => !!ud && x >= ud.x0 && x <= ud.x1;
305	  let x = rng.int(6, 14);
306	  let sinceLast = 0;
307	  while (x < w - 6) {
308	    let sy = -1;
309	    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
310	      if (st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) { sy = y; break; }
311	    }
312	    if (sy >= 0) {
313	      const ground = st.type[st.idx(x, sy)];
314	      const relaxed = sinceLast >= 24;
315	      // ---- 树已由 vanilla/TreePass.ts 替换（原版 AddTrees 逐列语义）----
316	      // ---- 仙人掌(沙漠沙/邪恶沙面)----
317	      if ((ground === T.SAND || ground === EBONSAND || ground === CRIMSAND) && inDesert(x)
318	        && !st.flags[st.idx(x, sy - 1)] && rng.next() < 0.12) {
319	        const ch = rng.int(2, 5);
320	        let ok = true;
321	        for (let dy = 1; dy <= ch; dy++) if (st.flags[st.idx(x, sy - dy)]) { ok = false; break; }
322	        if (ok) {
323	          for (let dy = 1; dy <= ch; dy++) st.setTileSilent(x, sy - dy, CACTUS, 0, 0);
324	          x += rng.int(4, 9);
325	          sinceLast = 0;
326	          continue;
327	        }
328	      }
329	      // ---- 杂草/花/蘑菇 ----(藤蔓在下方统一扫描)
330	      // ---- 杂草/花/蘑菇 ----
331	      if (!st.flags[st.idx(x, sy - 1)] && (ground === T.GRASS || ground === JUNGLE_GRASS || ground === T.SNOW)) {
332	        const r = rng.next();
333	        if (r < 0.3) st.setTileSilent(x, sy - 1, T.TALLGRASS, [0, 18, 36, 54, 72, 90][rng.int(0, 5)], 0);
334	        else if (r < 0.42) st.setTileSilent(x, sy - 1, T.FLOWER, [108, 126, 162, 180, 216, 252, 342, 540, 594][rng.int(0, 8)], 0);
335	        else if (r < 0.45 && ground !== T.SNOW) st.setTileSilent(x, sy - 1, T.MUSHROOM, 144, 0);
336	      }
337	    }
338	    x++;
339	    sinceLast++;
340	  }
341	  // ---- 藤蔓:挂在悬空草根/草皮下方(洞穴顶/崖底)----
342	  for (let vx = 2; vx < w - 2; vx++) {
343	    for (let vy = 2; vy < h - 2; vy++) {
344	      const i = st.idx(vx, vy);
345	      if (st.type[i] !== JUNGLE_GRASS && st.type[i] !== T.GRASS) continue;
346	      if (!st.flags[i] || st.flags[st.idx(vx, vy + 1)]) continue;
347	      if (st.liquid[st.idx(vx, vy + 1)] > 0) continue;
348	      if (rng.next() < 0.5) continue;
349	      const len = rng.int(1, 8);
350	      for (let k = 1; k <= len; k++) {
351	        const ii = st.idx(vx, vy + k);
352	        if (vy + k >= h - 1 || st.flags[ii] || st.type[ii] !== 0) break;
353	        st.setTileSilent(vx, vy + k, VINE, 0, 0);
354	      }
355	      vy += 10; // 稀疏
356	    }
357	  }
358	}
359	
360	const SANDSTONE_BRICK = TILE_BY_KEY['v_151_sandstone_brick']!;
361	const CHEST = TILE_BY_KEY['chest']!;
362	
363	/** 金字塔(pass 38 Pyramids 简化):gs.pyramidSpots 候选 → 地下沙岩砖金字塔 + 宝箱 */
364	export function runPyramidPass(
365	  st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState,
366	  chests: Array<{ x: number; y: number; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null> }>,
367	): void {
368	  for (const spot of gs.pyramidSpots.slice(0, 2)) {
369	    const size = rng.int(14, 20); // 半宽
370	    const baseY = Math.min(st.h - 260, spot.y + rng.int(60, 90));
371	    if (spot.x < size + 5 || spot.x > st.w - size - 5) continue;
372	    // 金字塔主体(逐层收窄)
373	    for (let layer = 0; layer < size; layer++) {
374	      const halfW = size - layer;
375	      for (let x = spot.x - halfW; x <= spot.x + halfW; x++) {
376	        for (let y = baseY - layer; y <= baseY - layer; y++) {
377	          const i = st.idx(x, y);
378	          st.type[i] = SANDSTONE_BRICK; st.flags[i] = 1;
379	          st.wall[i] = 34; // SandstoneBrickUnsafe
380	        }
381	      }
382	    }
383	    // 内腔(倒阶梯掏空)
384	    for (let layer = 2; layer < size - 2; layer++) {
385	      const halfW = size - layer - 2;
386	      for (let x = spot.x - halfW; x <= spot.x + halfW; x++) {
387	        const i = st.idx(x, baseY - layer);
388	        st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
389	      }
390	    }
391	    // 宝箱(内腔底部,cs L12417-12426):style1 金箱,战利品三选一
392	    // (857 沙暴瓶/848 法老面具/934 飞毯)
393	    const bx = spot.x, by = baseY - 2;
394	    st.setTileSilent(bx, by, CHEST, 1 * 36, 0);
395	    st.setTileSilent(bx + 1, by, CHEST, 1 * 36 + 18, 0);
396	    st.setTileSilent(bx, by + 1, CHEST, 1 * 36, 18);
397	    st.setTileSilent(bx + 1, by + 1, CHEST, 1 * 36 + 18, 18);
398	    const lootKeys = ['vi_857_sandstorm_in_a_bottle', 'vi_848_PharaohsMask', 'vi_934_FlyingCarpet'];
399	    const lootId = ITEM_BY_KEY[lootKeys[rng.int(0, 2)]];
400	    chests.push({
401	      x: bx, y: by,
402	      items: lootId !== undefined ? [{ id: lootId, stack: 1 }, ...Array(9).fill(null)] : Array(10).fill(null),
403	    });
404	  }
405	}
406	
407	/** Wet Jungle(pass 43 简化):从丛林草 tile 起挖湿隧道(灌水) */
408	export function runWetJunglePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
409	  const core = (gs as any).jungleCoreX ?? gs.jungleX;
410	  const JG = JUNGLE_GRASS;
411	  const tunnels = Math.max(4, Math.floor(st.w / 500)); // C# (int)
412	  let dug = 0;
413	  for (let n = 0; n < tunnels * 60 && dug < tunnels; n++) {
414	    const x = core + rng.int(-Math.floor(st.w * 0.12), Math.floor(st.w * 0.12));
415	    const y = rng.int(gs.rockLevel, Math.max(gs.rockLevel + 1, st.h - 500));
416	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== JG || !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) continue;
417	    const dx = rng.int(-100, 100) * 0.01, dy = rng.int(-100, 100) * 0.01;
418	    digTunnel(st, rng, x, y, dx, dy, rng.int(30, 120), rng.int(3, 6), true);
419	    dug++;
420	  }
421	}
422

424	const HONEY = TILE_BY_KEY['v_229_honey_block']!;
```

```
      if (isDWall(x, y) && placeTrap0(x, y)) { fail = 0; added++; }
      if (fail > 1000) { fail = 0; added++; }
    }
```

```
144	      if (bad) continue;
```

```
export function drawTreeCell(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: SpriteAtlas,
  st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, fx: number, fy: number, px: number, py: number,
  sheet = 5,
  foliageOnly = false,
  trees?: TreeStyleData,
): void {
  const style = TREE_SHEET_STYLES[sheet] ?? 0;
  // 棕榈树（tile 323）：树冠帧藏在树干列内（frameX 88-132，无独立标记格）。
  //  TEdit：Tree_Tops_15（80×80、82px 步长），srcX = 变体×82、srcY = 沙型×82，
  //  底部锚定 + X 偏移 frameY/16 格；沙型 = 下方沙块种类（普通0/猩红1/珍珠2/腐化3）
  if (sheet === 323) {
    if (fx >= 88 && fx <= 132) {
      if (!foliageOnly) return; // 树冠/干身全部由外扩遍绘制（倾斜跨列/跨 chunk 防裁剪）
      const img15 = atlas.ensureVImage('vanilla/Tree_Tops_15.png');
      if (!img15) return;
      // 沙型：向下扫沙块
      let palmType = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
        const ty = y + i;
        if (ty >= st.h) break;
        const t = st.type[st.idx(x, ty)];
        if (t === 0) continue;
        const tk = TILE_DEFS[t]?.key;
        palmType = tk === 'v_234_crimsand_block' ? 1 : tk === 'v_116_pearlsand_block' ? 2
          : tk === 'v_112_ebonsand_block' ? 3 : 0;
        break;
      }
      const v323 = Math.floor((fx - 88) / 22); // 0/1/2 变体
      const sx15 = v323 * 82, sy15 = palmType * 82;
      if (sx15 + 80 > img15.width || sy15 + 80 > img15.height) return;
      // 底部锚定居中 + X 偏移 = frameY【像素·有符号】（原版 dest.X += V*zoom/16，
      // zoom=16 即 V 像素；65534 = -2 的 u16 回绕）
      const leanCrown = fy >= 32768 ? fy - 65536 : fy;
      ctx.drawImage(img15, sx15, sy15, 80, 80,
        px + (TILE - 80) / 2 + leanCrown, py + TILE - 80, 80, 80);
      return;
    }
    // 干身（原版 TileDrawing case 323：20×20 帧 22px 步长，底部对齐；
    // frameY 是**有符号像素偏移**——棕榈树干随高度倾斜，65534 = -2 的 u16 回绕）
    const img323 = atlas.ensureVImage('vanilla/Tiles_323.png');
    if (!img323 || fx + 20 > img323.width) return;
    const lean = fy >= 32768 ? fy - 65536 : fy; // 原版 vector2.X += frameY
    const pad323 = treeFrameBottomPad(img323, fx, 0);
    const artH323 = 20 - pad323;
    ctx.drawImage(img323, fx, 0, 20, 20, px - 2 + lean, py + TILE - artH323, 20, 20);
    return;
  }
  // 蘑菇树（tile 72）：独立渲染路径——U≥36 标记帧 = 巨蘑菇顶（Shroom_Tops，60×42、62px 步长、底部锚定）
  if (sheet === 72) {
    if (foliageOnly === false && fx >= 36) return; // 顶格由外扩遍画
    if (fx >= 36) {
      const img72 = atlas.ensureVImage('vanilla/Shroom_Tops.png');
      if (img72 && fx + 60 <= img72.width && fy + 42 <= img72.height) {
        // TEdit：source.X = (V/18)*62；60×42 底部锚定居中
        const sx72 = Math.floor(fy / 18) * 62;
        if (sx72 + 60 <= img72.width) {
          ctx.drawImage(img72, sx72, 0, 60, 42, px + (TILE - 60) / 2, py + TILE - 42, 60, 42);
        }
      }
      return;
    }
    // 干身：Tiles_72 直取（54×54 表，2×2 帧 18px 网格）
    const img72 = atlas.ensureVImage('vanilla/Tiles_72.png');
    if (img72 && fx + 16 <= img72.width && fy + 16 <= img72.height) {
      ctx.drawImage(img72, fx, fy, 16, 16, px, py, TILE, TILE);
    }
    return;
  }
  if (fy >= 198 && fx >= 22) {
    // 树冠/树枝标记帧（仅由 ChunkCache 的外扩遍绘制，防跨 chunk 裁剪）
    if (foliageOnly === false) return;
    // 变体来源 = 标记帧自身 frameY（原版 WorldGen.GetTreeFrame：198→0/220→1/242→2）
    const frame = fy >= 242 ? 2 : fy >= 220 ? 1 : 0;
    // 样式选择（原版 GetCommonTreeFoliageData；树族 tile 用固定样式映射）
    // 树型扫描列：左枝在右侧树干列找地 / 右枝在左侧（原版 xoffset）
    const scanX = x + (fx === 44 ? 1 : fx === 66 ? -1 : 0);
    const scan = scanTreeType(st, scanX, y);
    const crown = sheet === 5 && trees
      ? crownStyleFor(scanX, scan.type, scan.floorY, trees)
      : style;
    let img: HTMLImageElement | null;
    let sx = 0, sy = 0, sw = 0, sh = 0, anchor = 0; // 0=底中(顶) 1=右中(左枝) 2=左中(右枝)
    if (fx === 22) {
      img = atlas.ensureVImage(`vanilla/Tree_Tops_${crown}.png`)
        ?? atlas.ensureVImage(`vanilla/Tree_Tops_${style}.png`) ?? null;
      if (!img) return;
      // 帧尺寸按 PNG 实宽推导（3 变体 + 2px 间隔），自动覆盖全部样式（含神圣 140 高）
      [sw, sh] = treeTopSize(img);
      sx = frame * (sw + 2); anchor = 0;
    } else {
      // 分叉枝（原版固定 40×40 帧、42px 步长；左枝 X=0 / 右枝 X=42；Y = frame*42）
      img = atlas.ensureVImage(`vanilla/Tree_Branches_${crown}.png`)
        ?? atlas.ensureVImage(`vanilla/Tree_Branches_${style}.png`) ?? null;
      if (!img) return;
      sw = 40; sh = 40;
      sx = fx === 44 ? 0 : 42;
      sy = frame * 42; anchor = fx === 44 ? 1 : 2;
    }
    if (sx + sw > img.width || sy + sh > img.height) return;
    let dx = px, dy = py;
    if (anchor === 0) { dx = px + (TILE - sw) / 2; dy = py + TILE - sh; }
    else if (anchor === 1) { dx = px + TILE - sw; dy = py + (TILE - sh) / 2; }
    else { dy = py + (TILE - sh) / 2; }
    ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, sw, sh, dx, dy, sw, sh);
    return;
  }
  if (foliageOnly) return; // 外扩遍只画树冠/树枝
  // 树干：TEdit baseX 奇偶修正（特殊帧横向偏一列扫草地）+ 100 格深扫描
  let baseX = 0;
  if (fx === 66 && fy <= 45) baseX++;
  if (fx === 88 && fy >= 66 && fy <= 110) baseX--;
  if (fx === 22 && fy >= 132 && fy < 198) baseX--;
  if (fx === 44 && fy >= 132 && fy < 198) baseX++;
  const type = scanTreeType(st, x + baseX, y).type;
  const img = type >= 0
```

```
41	      }
42	    }
43	    px += vx; py += vy;
44	    vx += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.05;
45	    vy += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.05;
46	    if (vx > 0.5) vx = 0.5;
47	    if (vx < -0.5) vx = -0.5;
48	    if (vy > -0.5) vy = -0.5;
49	    if (vy < -1.5) vy = -1.5;
50	  }
51	}

60	    let placed = false;
61	    while (!placed && !giveUp) {
62	      placed = true;
63	      while (x > st.w / 2 - 90 && x < st.w / 2 + 90) {
64	        x = rng.int(Math.floor(st.w * 0.25), Math.floor(st.w * 0.75) - 1);
65	      }
66	      for (let k = 0; k < gs.numMCaves; k++) {
67	        if (Math.abs(x - gs.mCaveX[k]) < 100) { tries++; placed = false; break; }
68	      }
69	      if (tries >= st.w / 5) { giveUp = true; break; }
70	    }
```

```
103	        if (!st.flags[i] || !st.flags[st.idx(x, y + 1)]) continue;
104	        if (!SANDS.has(st.type[i]) || !SANDS.has(st.type[st.idx(x, y + 1)])) continue;
105	        const nx = x + step;

107	        let ny = y + 1;

109	        const sandType = st.type[i]; // 先取后清
```

```json
    }
```

```
      let nearSand = false;

        for (let dy = -25; dy < 25; dy++) {
          const xx = x + dx, yy = y + dy;
          if (!st.inBounds(xx, yy) || !st.flags[st.idx(xx, yy)]) continue;
          if (SAND_AVOID.has(st.type[st.idx(xx, yy)])) { nearSand = true; break; }
        }
      }
      if (!nearSand) {
        mountinater(st, rng, x, y);
        gs.mCaveX[gs.numMCaves] = x;
        gs.mCaveY[gs.numMCaves] = y;
        gs.numMCaves++;
      }
      break; // 原版无论放没放都 break 出列扫描
    }

}

/** CaveOpenater（L48789）：山侧横向开口，100 步或直到离开墙/碰不可清 tile */
function caveOpenater(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, i: number, j: number): void {
  let num1 = rng.int(6, 11);              // Next(7,12)
  let dir = rng.int(0, 1) === 0 ? -1 : 1; // Next(2)
  if (rng.int(0, 9) !== 0) dir = i >= st.w / 2 ? -1 : 1; // 90% 朝世界外侧（Next(10)!=0）
  let px = i, py = j;
  let steps = 100;
  let vx = dir, vy = 0;
  while (steps > 0) {
    const k = st.idx(Math.max(0, Math.min(st.w - 1, ctrunc(px))), Math.max(0, Math.min(st.h - 1, ctrunc(py))));
    const t = st.type[k];
    if (st.wall[k] === 0 || (st.flags[k] && NOT_CLEARABLE.has(t))) steps = 0;
    steps--;
    let x0 = ctrunc(px - num1 * 0.5), x1 = ctrunc(px + num1 * 0.5);
    let y0 = ctrunc(py - num1 * 0.5), y1 = ctrunc(py + num1 * 0.5);
    if (x0 < 0) x0 = 0;
    if (x1 > st.w) x1 = st.w;
    if (y0 < 0) y0 = 0;
    if (y1 > st.h) y1 = st.h;
    const r = num1 * rng.int(79, 119) * 0.01;
    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
        const dx = Math.abs(x - px), dy = Math.abs(y - py);
        if (Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) < r * 0.4) {
          const kk = st.idx(x, y);
          if (st.flags[kk] && !NOT_CLEARABLE.has(st.type[kk])) st.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
        }
      }
    }
    px += vx; py += vy;
    vx += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.05;
    vy += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.05;
    if (vx > dir + 0.5) vx = dir + 0.5;
    if (vx < dir - 0.5) vx = dir - 0.5;
    if (vy > 0) vy = 0;
    if (vy < -0.5) vy = -0.5;
  }
}

/** Cavinator（L48733）：向下蜿蜒洞穴，递归 steps 次直至岩层（不挖沙 53） */
function cavinator(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number, steps: number): void {
  let num1 = rng.int(6, 14);              // Next(7,15)
  const dir = rng.int(0, 1) === 0 ? -1 : 1;
  let px = i, py = j;
  let count = rng.int(19, 39);            // Next(20,40)
  let vx = dir, vy = rng.int(10, 19) * 0.01; // Next(10,20)
  const SAND = TILE_BY_KEY['sand']!;
  while (count > 0) {
    count--;
    let x0 = ctrunc(px - num1 * 0.5), x1 = ctrunc(px + num1 * 0.5);
    let y0 = ctrunc(py - num1 * 0.5), y1 = ctrunc(py + num1 * 0.5);
    if (x0 < 0) x0 = 0;
    if (x1 > st.w) x1 = st.w;
    if (y0 < 0) y0 = 0;
    if (y1 > st.h) y1 = st.h;
    const r = num1 * rng.int(79, 119) * 0.01;
    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
        const dx = Math.abs(x - px), dy = Math.abs(y - py);
        if (Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) < r * 0.4) {
          const kk = st.idx(x, y);
          if (st.flags[kk] && !NOT_CLEARABLE.has(st.type[kk]) && st.type[kk] !== SAND) {
            st.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    px += vx; py += vy;
    vx += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.05;
    vy += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.05;
    if (vx > dir + 0.5) vx = dir + 0.5;
    if (vx < dir - 0.5) vx = dir - 0.5;
    if (vy > 2) vy = 2;
    if (vy < 0) vy = 0;
  }
  if (steps <= 0 || ctrunc(py) >= gs.rockLevel + 50) return;
  cavinator(st, rng, gs, ctrunc(px), ctrunc(py), steps - 1);
}

/** pass 34 Mountain Caves（L6628-6639）：每座山丘 开口+蜿蜒 */
export function runMountainCavesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  for (let n = 0; n < gs.numMCaves; n++) {
    const x = gs.mCaveX[n], y = gs.mCaveY[n];
    caveOpenater(st, rng, x, y);
    cavinator(st, rng, gs, x, y, rng.int(39, 49)); // Next(40,50)
  }
}

```

````
| 位置 | key | 影响 |

## 4. 火焰陷阱 tile id — ✗（当前实现用错 tile）

**cs `placeTrap` type==1 分支（WorldGen.cs:3466-3537）原文核对：**

```
L3506  PlaceTile(index3,   y+2, 130, ...)   ← 火焰陷阱体（左列）
L3507  PlaceTile(index3+1, y+2, 130, ...)   ← 火焰陷阱体（右列）
L3508  PlaceTile(index3+1, y+1, 138, ...)   ← 顶部件（仅右列、y+1 一格）
L3513-3518  y+3、y+4 两行各 2 格，均为 130
```

即**火焰陷阱 = tile 130（6 格）+ tile 138（顶部 1 格）**，不是 137，也不是 130/138 混用的"kind2 frameY=36"。

**`TrapsPass.ts` 当前实现的问题（全部在 type===1 分支，L63-102）：**

1. **L92-97 用 `TRAP`（=v_137_traps，飞镖陷阱 tile）+ frameY=36 写全部陷阱体** → tile id 错。应改为 sheet 130（注意：tiles.ts 里 `v_130_active_stone_block`，名字是 Active Stone，需先确认该 sheet 在本项目贴图表里的语义）与 sheet 138（`v_138_boulder`）。
2. **缺顶部件**：cs 在 `(colX+1, y+1)` 放 138；`TrapsPass.ts:93-97` 只写 y+2..y+4 三行，漏 y+1。
3. **帧值错**：cs `PlaceTile(..., -1, 0)` style 0 → frameX=0/frameY=0；`TrapsPass` 用 frameY=36 无依据。
4. **L85-89 周壁转大理石（v_367_marble_block）**：cs L3489-3496 是把 6×6 内实心格 `type = 1`（**石头**），不是大理石。
5. **缺前置检查**：cs L3469 `if (IsTileNearby(index3, y, 138, 10)) return false`（TrapsPass 无）；cs L3358-3366 type==1 时若 (px±3, py±3) 内有 tile 147（雪块）/161（冰）则降级为 type 0——TrapsPass 无此降级，雪原会出火焰陷阱。
6. ✓ 正确项：压板 style 7 → frameX 126（L99，对应 cs `PlaceTile(...,135,...,7)`）；竖井扫描/掏空/L 布线骨架与 cs 同构。

**type 0 飞镖 vs `DungeonPass.ts` `placeTrap0`（L1092-1160）差异核对：** 核心逻辑（下扫、蘑菇草 20 格/墙 87 回避、3×3×2 净空、尖刺 48/232 回避、`j2 = py - Next(3)`、左右扫锚 5..49、锚下实心、门/尖刺坏锚、双锚随机选边、发光蘑菇 190 回避、压板 style、飞镖 frameX 18/0、L 形三段 wire）**逐条一致** ✓。差异仅两处：
- `placeTrap0` 无 `deep`/type 随机段（地牢场景恒有墙，lava 直接 return），合理；
- `TrapsPass.ts:192-196 isCracked` 用 `?? -1` 处理 482（因 key 缺失，绿裂砖永不命中）；DungeonPass 用 `.filter(t => t != null)`——行为等价但同样受 482 缺 def 影响。
- 另：type 2 的压板 style，cs L3571 恒为 `Next(2,4)`，`TrapsPass.ts:118` 却按 `wall>0 ? 36 : Next(2,4)`——轻微偏差。

## 5. TreePass 细节 — ✓ 帧表核对通过（含 1 个消费端渲染断点 + RNG 契约偏差）

**growTrunk 帧表抽查 3 处（对照 cs L14213-14606）——全部一致：**
- 干身 case 1：`(0,66)/(0,88)/(0,110)` ✓（cs L14258-14273）；case 5：`(88,0)/(88,22)/(88,44)` ✓；case 7：`(110,66)/(110,88)/(110,110)` ✓。TreePass.ts:26-36 的 TRUNK 表 0-7 全对。
- 侧枝：左枝主支 `(44, 198+22k)`、次支 `(66, k*22)` ✓；右枝主支 `(66, 198+22k)`、次支 `(88, 66+22k)` ✓（cs L14362-14443，含 `Next(3)<2` 概率分支）。
- 基座/根部：右根 `(22, 132+22k)`、左根 `(44, 132+22k)`、基座干 num7 0/1/2 → fx 88/0/66、num7=3 不写 ✓；树冠 `(fx=22|0, fy=198+22k)`，`Next(13)!=0` 取 22 ✓（cs L14568-14601）。

**GrowPalmTree（cs L11785-11847）**：`frameY = tilt`（±2 步进趋向 `Next(-8,9)*2`）、基座 fx=66、顶段 `fx=22*Next(4,7)`、干身 `fx=22*Next(0,3)`、倾斜阈值 0.25/0.5/0.7/0.95 + `|| true` 死条件，全部与 cs 一致 ✓。

**消费端 `VanillaTiler.ts drawTreeCell`（L215 起）匹配性：**
- ✓ 棕榈：`fy >= 32768 ? fy - 65536 : fy` 正确处理 Uint16Array 负 tilt 回绕（TileStore.ts:10 `frameY: Uint16Array`）；冠判定 `fx>=88 && fx<=132` 与 TreePass 顶段 fx∈{88,110,132} 匹配。
- ✗ **普通树冠 fx=0 断点**：TreePass.ts:94 有 1/13 概率写 `crownFx = 0`（忠实原版），但渲染端 `VanillaTiler.ts:288` 与 `ChunkCache.ts:308` 的冠判定都是 `fy >= 198 && fx >= 22`——fx=0 的树冠格既不进外扩冠绘制，又会在主遍落进树干分支按 Tiles_5(0,198) 画错图。原版 `GetTreeFrame`（cs L37498）**只看 frameY**。修复：渲染端冠判定去掉 `fx >= 22` 条件（改为 `fy >= 198` 即冠/枝标记，fx 仅用于区分 22=顶、44/66=枝），或生成端钳制 fx=22（偏离原版但省事）。

**其他 TreePass 偏差（次要）：**
- RNG 摸取顺序与 cs 不同：cs 每格先 `frameNumber(Next(3))`（未移植）再 `num4=Next(3)` 再 `num5=Next(10)`；TreePass.ts:55-63 先 num5 再 num4、且无 frameNumber 摸取。帧值分布等价但**同种子结果与原版发散**，RNG 契约不可复现。
- 根/基座变体：cs 分别摸 3 次（右根 num4、左根 num4、num8），TreePass.ts:87 只摸 1 次 `k8` 复用。
- `growTree` 缺 cs L14203 的蘑菇草(70)特例（额外 `EmptyTileCheck(i-1,i+1,index1-2,index1-1)`）。
- `FIT` 集合 9 项中 sheet **492 无 def**（tiles.ts 缺）；`PALM_GROUND` 中 sheet **116（珍珠沙）无 def** → 神圣海滩不长棕榈。均为数据缺口而非逻辑错。
- 柳/樱观赏树未移植——已在 TreePass.ts:174 注释声明，✓（文档化缺口）。
- `randomizeTreeStyle` 与 cs L2817-2870 逐分支一致（4200/6400/default 三档、互异重掷、style0→4 的 2/3 概率、调用时机在 dungeonSide 之前）✓；`AddTrees` 逐列/跳列/双端棕榈概率（0.1+NextFloat*0.35）与 cs L15642-15681 一致 ✓。

## 6. 死代码 — ✗（多处）

1. **`StructuresPass.ts:423-425`**：`HIVE`/`HONEY`/`COBWEB` 三个 const 无任何引用——蜂巢/蛛网逻辑迁去 `HiveSpiderPass.ts` 后的孤儿，删。
2. **`StructuresPass.ts:247 runHellHousesPass`**：导出无调用（被 `HellFortPass` 取代），连同 L13-21 的 `CLOUD`/`OBSIDIAN_BRICK`/`HELL_BRICK` 部分依赖一起评估删除。
3. **`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/world/gen/WorldGe`**（472 行，无扩展名）：WorldGen.ts 的旧副本死文件，删。
4. **陈旧 .js 构建产物 + Vite 解析顺序风险（最高优先级）**：`vanilla/` 下每个 .ts 都带旧 .js；`LakesPass.ts`/`TrapsPass.ts`/`GenState.ts`(新)/`StructuresPass.ts`(新) 甚至没有对应 .js 或 .js 落后（WorldGen.ts 12:24 vs WorldGen.js 11:38）。Vite 默认 `resolve.extensions` 是 `.js` 先于 `.ts`——**运行时很可能加载的是旧 WorldGen.js（仍含 liquidPass、无 Lakes/Traps）**。建议整体清除 src 下手写 .js 或在 vite config 里把 `.ts` 提前。
5. `Spread.ts spreadGrass`、`HalfBrickPass.ts solidTile` 未用导出。
6. `TrapsPass.ts:6 与 :10` 重复 import `TileStore`（`TileStore` 与 `TileStore as TS`），合并为一条。
7. `TrapsPass.ts:3` 头注释「1/20 巨石」应为「1/20 炸药(141)」（type 2 实际是 cs L3540-3585 的 explosives）。
````
